) and invoking the union bound, we conclude that: with probability exceeding
1-O(d^{-20})
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\Big\|\Big(\big(p^{-1}\mathcal{P}_{\Omega_{m}}-\mathcal{P}_{m}\big)\big(\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\big)\Big)\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\Big\|_{\mathrm{F}}=\big\|\bm{V}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq C\frac{\mu^{3/2}r\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\sqrt{\log d}}{d\sqrt{p}}\,\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{2,\infty}.\end{split}

(129)

for some absolute constant
C>0
, where we use the assumption that
\kappa\asymp 1
.

(3) For
\alpha_{3}
, following a similar argument for
\alpha_{2}
, we define

\displaystyle\bm{W}^{t,\left(m\right)}

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{P}_{\Omega_{m}}\left(\bm{E}\right)\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}

\displaystyle\bm{w}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{P}_{\Omega_{m}}\left(\bm{E}\right)\times_{1}\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\times_{2}\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}

for each
s\in[r]
. The
m
-th row of
\bm{W}^{t,\left(m\right)}
is a sum of independent zero-mean random vectors:

\displaystyle\bm{W}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{m,:}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,j\in[d]}\sum_{s\in[r]}\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{i}\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{j}E_{i,j,m}\chi_{i,j,m}\bm{e}_{s}^{\top}.

With (
119
) in place, it is easy to verify that

\displaystyle\max_{i,j\in[d]}\Big\|\sum\nolimits_{s\in[r]}\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{i}\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{j}E_{i,j,m}\chi_{i,j,m}\bm{e}_{s}^{\top}\Big\|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma\max_{s\in[r]}\big\|\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big\|_{\infty}\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{d}}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star 1/3}\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}=:L_{2}

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j\in[d]}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\Big\|\sum\nolimits_{s\in[r]}\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{i}\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{j}E_{i,j,m}\chi_{i,j,m}\bm{e}_{s}^{\top}\Big\|_{2}^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,j\in[d]}\sum_{s\in[r]}\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{i}^{2}\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{j}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\left(E_{i,j,m}\chi_{i,j,m}\right)^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{2}p\max_{s\in[r]}\big\|\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{2}\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}dp\lambda_{\max}^{\star 2/3}\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}^{2}=:V_{2},

where we have used (
119
) and the fact that
\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\sqrt{d}\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}
.
Apply the matrix Bernstein inequality to reveal that: with probability at least
1-O(d^{-20})
,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{W}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{m,:}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Big\|\sum_{i,j\in[d]}\sum_{s\in[r]}\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{i}\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{j}E_{i,j,m}\chi_{i,j,m}\bm{e}_{s}^{\top}\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim L_{2}\log^{2}d+\sqrt{V_{2}\log d}\asymp\sigma\lambda_{\max}^{\star 1/3}\sqrt{dp\log d}\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty},

(130)

where the last inequality holds as long as
p\gg\mu d^{-2}\log^{3}d
.

As for the other rows, we have

\displaystyle\big(\bm{w}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{k}

\displaystyle=\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{m}\sum_{j\in[d]}\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{j}E_{m,j,k}\chi_{m,j,k}+\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{m}\sum_{i:i\neq m}\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{i}E_{i,m,k}\chi_{i,m,k}

for each
s\in[r]
. Arguing similarly as in the proof of Lemma
D.10
,
we have with probability at least
1-O(d^{-20})
,

\displaystyle\sum_{s\in[r]}\sum_{k:k\neq m}

\displaystyle\Big(\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{m}\sum\nolimits_{j\in[d]}\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{j}E_{m,j,k}\chi_{m,j,k}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{s\in[r]}\sum_{k:k\neq m}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{m}\sum_{j\in[d]}\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{j}\left(E_{m,j,k}\chi_{m,j,k}\right)\Big)^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{2}dp\sum_{s\in[r]}\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{m}^{2}\sum_{j\in[d]}\big(\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{2}dp\max_{s\in[r]}\big\|\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{2}\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\lesssim\sigma^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{\star 2/3}dp\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}^{2},

where the last inequality follows from (
119
). Additionally, the summation over
\{i:i\neq m\}
can be controlled using the same argument. Therefore, we use Cauchy-Schwarz to find that

\displaystyle\sum_{k:k\neq m}\left\|\bm{W}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{k,:}\right\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim\sigma^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{\star 2/3}dp\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}^{2}.

(131)

Combined with (
130
) and the assumption that
\kappa\asymp 1
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\left\|p^{-1}\mathcal{P}_{\Omega_{m}}\left(\bm{E}\right)\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}=\big\|\bm{W}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\widetilde{C}\sigma\lambda_{\min}^{\star 1/3}\sqrt{dp\log d}\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty},

(132)

for some absolute constant
\widetilde{C}>0
.

(4) Regarding
\alpha_{4}
, we use the triangle inequality to show that for each
s\in[r]
,

\displaystyle\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\Omega}\left(\bm{E}\right)\times_{1}\bm{u}^{t}_{s}\times_{2}\bm{u}^{t}_{s}

\displaystyle-\mathcal{P}_{\Omega}\left(\bm{E}\right)\times_{1}\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\times_{2}\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\Omega}\left(\bm{E}\right)\times_{1}\big(\bm{u}^{t}_{s}-\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)\times_{2}\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big\|_{2}+\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\Omega}\left(\bm{E}\right)\times_{1}\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\times_{2}\big(\bm{u}^{t}_{s}-\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\Omega}\left(\bm{E}\right)\times_{1}\big(\bm{u}^{t}_{s}-\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)\times_{2}\big(\bm{u}^{t}_{s}-\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\Omega}\left(\bm{E}\right)\right\|\big\|\bm{u}^{t}_{s}-\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big\|_{2}\big\|\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big\|_{2}+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\Omega}\left(\bm{E}\right)\right\|\big\|\bm{u}^{t}_{s}-\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\Omega}\left(\bm{E}\right)\right\|\big\|\bm{u}^{t}_{s}-\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big\|_{2}\big\|\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big\|_{2},

where the last line follows from (
119
). From Corollary
D.3
, we can further upper bound

\displaystyle\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\Omega}\left(\bm{E}\right)\times_{1}\bm{u}^{t}_{s}\times_{2}\bm{u}^{t}_{s}-\mathcal{P}_{\Omega}\left(\bm{E}\right)\times_{1}\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\times_{2}\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma\big(\sqrt{dp}+\log d\big)\log^{5/2}d\,\big\|\bm{u}^{t}_{s}-\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big\|_{2}\big\|\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big\|_{2}.

As a result, we sum over
s\in[r]
and use the Cauchy-Schwartz inequality to derive

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p}\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\Omega}\left(\bm{E}\right)\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t}

\displaystyle-\mathcal{P}_{\Omega}\left(\bm{E}\right)\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma}{p}\big(\sqrt{dp}+\log d\big)\log^{5/2}d\,\big\|\bm{U}^{t}-\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma}{p}\big(\sqrt{dp}+\log d\big)\log^{5/2}d\left(C_{5}\rho^{t}\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{local}}+C_{6}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}\right)\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\ll\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\left(C_{5}\rho^{t}\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{local}}+C_{6}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}\right)\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty},

(133)

where the last step arises from conditions that
\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}\ll\frac{1}{\mu^{3/2}r}
and
\kappa\asymp 1
.

(5) Taking (
126
), (
129
), (
132
) and (
133
) together, we can invoke the sample size assumption that
p\gg\mu^{3}r^{2}d^{-2}\log^{3}d
and the union bound to show that: with probability greater than
1-O(d^{-10})
one has

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{U}^{t+1}

\displaystyle-\bm{U}^{t+1,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\big(1-\frac{1}{4}\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\eta\big)\big\|\bm{U}^{t}-\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}+C\frac{\mu^{3/2}r\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\sqrt{\log d}}{d\sqrt{p}}\,\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+\widetilde{C}\eta\sigma\lambda_{\min}^{\star 1/3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu rd\log d}{p}}\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}+o\left(1\right)\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\eta\left(C_{5}\rho^{t}\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{local}}+C_{6}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}\right)\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\big(1-\frac{1}{4}\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\eta\big)\left(C_{5}\rho^{t}\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{local}}+C_{6}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}\right)\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\widetilde{C}\eta\sigma\lambda_{\min}^{\star 1/3}\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+C\eta\lambda_{\max}^{\star 4/3}\frac{\mu^{3/2}r\sqrt{\log d}}{d\sqrt{p}}\left(2C_{3}\rho^{t}\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{local}}+2C_{4}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}\right)\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+o\left(1\right)\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\eta\left(C_{5}\rho^{t}\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{local}}+C_{6}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu rd\log d}{p}}\right)\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\big(1-\frac{1}{5}\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\eta\big)C_{5}\rho^{t}\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{local}}\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left(\big(1-\frac{1}{5}\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\eta\big)C_{6}+\widetilde{C}\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\eta\right)\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\big(1-\frac{1}{5}\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\eta\big)C_{5}\rho^{t}\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{local}}\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}+C_{6}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left(C_{5}\rho^{t+1}\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{local}}+C_{6}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}\right)\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty},

provided
0<\eta\leq\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}/\big(32\lambda_{\max}^{\star 8/3}\big)
,
1-\big(\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}/5\big)\eta\leq\rho<1
and
C_{6}
is sufficiently large.

A.5
Proof of Lemma
5.5

Fix an arbitrary
m\in[d]
. Recall our notation of
\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}
in (
124
).
To simplify presentation, we further define

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{U}}^{t+1,\left(m\right)}

\displaystyle:=\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\eta\left(p^{-1}\mathcal{P}_{\Omega_{-m}}\big(\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}-\bm{E}\big)\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}+\mathcal{P}_{m}\big(\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\big)\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\right),

(134)

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}

\displaystyle:=\bm{u}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}-\bm{u}^{\star}_{s},

(135)

for each
s\in[r]
.

Apply the triangle inequality to yield

\displaystyle\big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t+1,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\big\|\big(\widehat{\bm{U}}^{t+1,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}}_{=:\,\alpha_{1}}+\underbrace{\big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t+1,\left(m\right)}-\widehat{\bm{U}}^{t+1,\left(m\right)}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}}_{=:\,\alpha_{2}},

leaving us with two terms to deal with. As it turns out, we will show that
\alpha_{1}
is the dominant term and
\alpha_{2}
is negligible. To simplify presentation, we shall assume that
\{E_{i,j,k}\}_{i,j,k\in[d]}
(resp.
\{\chi_{i,j,k}\}_{i,j,k\in[d]}
) are independent random variables.

•

Regarding
\alpha_{1}
, the definition of
\mathcal{P}_{\Omega_{-m}}
allows us to derive

\displaystyle\big(\widehat{\bm{U}}^{t+1,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}

\displaystyle=\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}-\eta\,\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}

\displaystyle=\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}-\eta\,\big(\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}.

We can express
\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}=\sum_{s\in[r]}\big(\bm{\Delta}_{s}^{t,\left(m\right)}+\bm{u}_{s}^{\star}\big)^{\otimes 3}-\bm{u}_{s}^{\star\otimes 3}
and compute that

\displaystyle\big(\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\times_{1}\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\times_{2}\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big)_{m}

\displaystyle\qquad=\Big(\big\|\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{2}+\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle\Big)^{2}\big(\bm{\Delta}_{s}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big)_{m}+\Big(2\,\big\|\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{2}\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle+\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle^{2}\Big)\big(\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big)_{m}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{i:i\neq s}\Big(\big\langle\bm{u}^{\star}_{i},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle+\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle\Big)^{2}\big(\bm{\Delta}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big)_{m}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{i:i\neq s}\Big(2\,\big\langle\bm{u}^{\star}_{i},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle+\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle^{2}\Big)\big(\bm{u}^{\star}_{i}\big)_{m}

(136)

for each
s\in[r]
. This further indicates that

\displaystyle\left\|\big(\widehat{\bm{U}}^{t+1,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\Big\|\sum\nolimits_{s\in[r]}\Big(1-\eta\left(\big\|\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{2}+\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle\right)^{2}\Big)\big(\bm{\Delta}_{s}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big)_{m}\bm{e}_{s}^{\top}\Big\|_{2}}_{=:\,\beta_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\eta\,\Big\|\sum\nolimits_{s\in[r]}\Big(2\,\big\|\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{2}\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle+\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle^{2}\Big)\big(\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big)_{m}\bm{e}_{s}^{\top}\Big\|_{2}}_{=:\,\beta_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\eta\,\Big\|\sum\nolimits_{s\in[r]}\sum\nolimits_{i:i\neq s}\Big(\big\langle\bm{u}^{\star}_{i},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle+\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle\Big)^{2}\big(\bm{\Delta}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big)_{m}\bm{e}_{s}^{\top}\Big\|_{2}}_{=:\,\beta_{3}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\eta\,\Big\|\sum\nolimits_{s\in[r]}\sum\nolimits_{i:i\neq s}\Big(2\,\big\langle\bm{u}^{\star}_{i},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle+\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle^{2}\Big)\left(\bm{u}^{\star}_{i}\right)_{m}\bm{e}_{s}^{\top}\Big\|_{2}}_{=:\,\beta_{4}}.

In what follows, we will control the four terms separately.

–

For
\beta_{1}
, by (
119
), we use Cauchy-Schwarz to show that

\displaystyle\Big(\big\|\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{2}+\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle\Big)^{2}\geq\left(\big\|\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{2}-\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s}\big\|_{2}\big\|\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\|_{2}\right)^{2}\geq\frac{2}{3}\big\|\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{4}\geq\frac{2}{3}\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}

for each
s\in[r]
. It follows that

\displaystyle\beta_{1}\leq\big(1-\frac{2}{3}\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\eta\big)\left\|\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\right\|_{2}.

(137)

–

Regarding
\beta_{2}
, by (
119
), we apply the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality again to get that: for each
s\in[r]
,

\displaystyle\Big|\Big(2\,\big\|\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{2}\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle+\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle^{2}\Big)\big(\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big)_{m}\Big|\leq 3\,\big\|\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{3}\big\|\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\|_{\infty}\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s}\big\|_{2}\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{d}}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star 4/3}\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s}\big\|_{2},

Together with the assumption that
\kappa\asymp 1
, this implies that

\displaystyle\beta_{2}\leq 3\eta\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{d}}\,\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

(138)

–

With regards to
\beta_{3}
, we show that for each
s\in[r]
:

\displaystyle\Big|

\displaystyle\sum\nolimits_{i:i\neq s}\Big(\big\langle\bm{u}^{\star}_{i},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle+\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle\Big)^{2}\big(\bm{\Delta}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big)_{m}\Big|

\displaystyle\;\;\lesssim\max_{i:i\neq s}\big|\big\langle\bm{u}^{\star}_{i},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle\big|^{2}\sum_{i:i\neq s}\left|\big(\bm{\Delta}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big)_{m}\right|+\big\|\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{2}\sum_{i:i\neq s}\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i}\big\|_{2}^{2}\left|\big(\bm{\Delta}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big)_{m}\right|

\displaystyle\;\;\leq\lambda_{\max}^{\star 4/3}\frac{\mu\sqrt{r}}{d}\big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}+\lambda_{\max}^{\star 2/3}\max_{i:i\neq s}\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i}\big\|_{2}\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}\big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\;\;\leq\lambda_{\max}^{\star 4/3}\Big(\frac{\mu\sqrt{r}}{d}+o\left(1/r\right)\Big)\big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}\ll\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{\star 4/3}}{\sqrt{r}}\big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2},

where the last line follows from (
119
) that
\max_{i:i\neq s}\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i}\big\|_{2}\leq\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}\ll\lambda_{\max}^{\star 1/3}/\sqrt{r}
and the low rank condition
r\ll\sqrt{d/\mu}
.
Summing over
s\in[r]
, we get

\displaystyle\beta_{3}\ll\eta\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\left\|\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\right\|_{2}

(139)

under the condition
\kappa\asymp 1
.

–

Turning attention to
\beta_{4}
, we observe that for each
s\in[r]
,

\displaystyle\Big|\sum\nolimits_{i:i\neq s}\Big(2\,\big\langle\bm{u}^{\star}_{i},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle+\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle^{2}\Big)\left(\bm{u}^{\star}_{i}\right)_{m}\Big|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\max_{i:i\neq s}\big|\big\langle\bm{u}^{\star}_{i},\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\rangle\big|\big\|\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\|_{2}\sum\nolimits_{i:i\neq s}\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i}\big\|_{2}\left|\big(\bm{u}_{i}^{\star}\big)_{m}\right|+\big\|\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{2}\sum\nolimits_{i:i\neq s}\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i}\big\|_{2}^{2}\left|\big(\bm{u}_{i}^{\star}\big)_{m}\right|

\displaystyle\quad\leq\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{d}}\,\big\|\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\lambda_{\max}^{\star 2/3}\max_{i:i\neq s}\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i}\big\|_{2}\,\big\|\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d}}\,\Big(\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{d}}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star 1/3}+o(1/\sqrt{r})\Big)\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}\ll\lambda_{\max}^{\star 4/3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{d}}\,\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}},

where the last line follows from (
119
) and the rank assumption
r\ll\sqrt{d/\mu}
. As a consequence,

\displaystyle\beta_{4}\ll\eta\lambda_{\max}^{\star 4/3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d}}\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

(140)

–

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\big(\widehat{\bm{U}}^{t+1,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big(1-\frac{1}{3}\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\eta\big)\left\|\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\right\|_{2}+4\eta\lambda_{\max}^{\star 4/3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d}}\,\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

(141)

•

With regards to
\alpha_{2}
, it follows from the definition (
46
) and (
134
) that

\displaystyle\bm{U}^{t+1,\left(m\right)}-\widehat{\bm{U}}^{t+1,\left(m\right)}

\displaystyle=-\eta\left(\left(p^{-1}\mathcal{P}_{\Omega_{-m}}+\mathcal{P}_{m}\right)\big(\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\big)\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\left(p^{-1}\mathcal{P}_{\Omega_{-m}}+\mathcal{P}_{m}\right)\big(\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\big)\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta}{p}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\Omega_{-m}}\left(\bm{E}\right)\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\mathcal{P}_{\Omega_{-m}}\left(\bm{E}\right)\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\right).

(142)

Recall the definition of
\mathcal{P}_{\Omega_{-m}}
and
\mathcal{P}_{m}
. For the
m
-th row, we have

\displaystyle\big(\bm{U}^{t+1,\left(m\right)}-\widehat{\bm{U}}^{t+1,\left(m\right)}\big)_{m,:}=-\eta\big(\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}

From the triangle inequality, we can further decompose

\displaystyle\Big\|\big(\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\underbrace{\left\|\big(\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\right\|_{2}}_{=:\gamma_{1}}+\underbrace{\left\|\big(\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)\big)_{m,:}\right\|_{2}}_{=:\gamma_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\underbrace{\left\|\big(\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)\big)_{m,:}\right\|_{2}}_{=:\gamma_{3}}.

Let us consider
\gamma_{1}
first. It is straightforward to calculate that

\displaystyle\big(\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\times_{1}\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\times_{2}\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big)_{m}=\sum_{i\in[r]}\big\langle\bm{u}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)},\bm{\Delta}_{s}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\rangle\big\langle\bm{u}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)},\bm{u}_{s}^{\star}\big\rangle\big(\bm{\Delta}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big)_{m}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i\in[r]}\Big(\big\langle\bm{\Delta}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)},\bm{\Delta}_{s}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\rangle\big\langle\bm{u}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)},\bm{u}_{s}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\rangle+\big\langle\bm{u}_{i}^{\star},\bm{\Delta}_{s}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\rangle\big\langle\bm{\Delta}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)},\bm{u}_{s}^{\star}\big\rangle+\big\langle\bm{\Delta}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)},\bm{\Delta}_{s}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\rangle\big\langle\bm{\Delta}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)},\bm{u}_{s}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\rangle\Big)\big(\bm{u}_{i}^{\star}\big)_{m}.

for each
s\in[r]
.
From (
119
), we use the triangle inequality and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality to upper bound

\displaystyle\Big|\big(

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\times_{1}\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\times_{2}\bm{u}^{\star}_{s}\big)_{m}\Big|\lesssim\big\|\bm{\Delta}_{s}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}\left\|\bm{u}_{s}^{\star}\right\|_{2}\sum_{i\in[r]}\big\|\bm{u}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}^{2}\left|\big(\bm{\Delta}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big)_{m}\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\big\|\bm{\Delta}_{s}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}\big(\left\|\bm{u}_{s}^{\star}\right\|_{2}+\big\|\bm{u}_{s}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}\big)\sum_{i\in[r]}\big\|\bm{\Delta}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}\big(\big\|\bm{u}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}+\left\|\bm{u}_{i}^{\star}\right\|_{2}+\big\|\bm{\Delta}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}\big)\left|\left(\bm{u}^{\star}_{i}\right)_{m}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{\max}^{\star 2/3}\big\|\bm{\Delta}_{s}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}\sum_{i\in[r]}\big\|\bm{u}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}\left|\big(\bm{\Delta}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big)_{m}\right|+\lambda_{\max}^{\star 2/3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{d}}\,\big\|\bm{\Delta}_{s}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}\sum_{i\in[r]}\big\|\bm{\Delta}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\bm{u}_{i}^{\star}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{\max}^{\star 2/3}\big\|\bm{\Delta}_{s}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}\big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}+\lambda_{\max}^{\star 2/3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{d}}\,\big\|\bm{\Delta}_{s}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\sqrt{r}\,\big\|\bm{\Delta}_{s}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}+\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d}}\,\big\|\bm{\Delta}_{s}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}

We then sum over
s\in[r]
to find that

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\right)_{m,:}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\sqrt{r}\,\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}\big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d}}\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2},

(143)

Moreover, it is easy to see that the upper bound also holds for
\gamma_{2}
. As for
\gamma_{3}
, we can express

\displaystyle\big(\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\times_{1}\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\times_{2}\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\big)_{m}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in[r]}\Big(2\,\big\langle\bm{u}^{\star}_{i},\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s}\big\rangle\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i},\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s}\big\rangle+\big\langle\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i},\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s}\big\rangle^{2}\Big)\left(\bm{u}^{\star}_{i}\right)_{m}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i\in[r]}\big\langle\bm{u}^{t,(m)}_{i},\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s}\big\rangle^{2}\big(\bm{\Delta}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big)_{m}.

(144)

Similarly, we combine (
119
) wtih the triangle inequality and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality to bound

\displaystyle\Big|\Big(\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\times_{1}\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\times_{2}\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{s}\Big)_{m}\Big|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{2}\sum_{i\in[r]}\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i}\big\|_{2}\left\|\bm{u}^{\star}_{i}\right\|_{2}\left|\left(\bm{u}^{\star}_{i}\right)_{m}\right|+\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{2}\sum_{i\in[r]}\big\|\bm{u}^{t,(m)}_{i}\big\|_{2}^{2}\left|\big(\bm{\Delta}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big)_{m}\right|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\lambda_{\max}^{\star 1/3}\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{2}\sum_{i\in[r]}\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{i}\big\|_{2}\left|\left(\bm{u}^{\star}_{i}\right)_{m}\right|+\lambda_{\max}^{\star 1/3}\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{2}\sum_{i\in[r]}\big\|\bm{u}^{t,(m)}_{i}\big\|_{2}\left|\big(\bm{\Delta}_{i}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big)_{m}\right|

\displaystyle\quad\leq\lambda_{\max}^{\star 1/3}\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{2}\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\lambda_{\max}^{\star 1/3}\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s}\big\|_{2}^{2}\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}\big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\lambda_{\max}^{\star 1/3}\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\lambda_{\max}^{\star 1/3}\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}\big\|\bm{\Delta}^{t,(m)}_{s}\big\|_{2}.

Sum over
s\in[r]
to obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)\right)_{m,:}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\lambda_{\max}^{\star 1/3}\sqrt{r}\,\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}+\lambda_{\max}^{\star 1/3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d}}\,\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{3}.

Since
\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}\ll\lambda_{\max}^{\star 1/3}/\sqrt{r}\leq 1
by (
119
), combined with (
143
), we find that

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{\Delta}^{t,\left(m\right)}_{\bm{T}}\times_{1}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\times_{2}^{\mathsf{seq}}\bm{U}^{\star}\right)_{m,:}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\lambda_{\max}^{\star 1/3}\sqrt{r}\,\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}\big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}+\lambda_{\max}^{\star 1/3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d}}\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\ll\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}+\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d}}\,\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

(145)

where the last inequality follows from the assumption that
\kappa\asymp 1
.

•

Putting (
141
) and (
145
) together, we reach the conclusion from (
48
) and the condition
r\ll\sqrt{d/\mu}
that,

\displaystyle\Big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t+1,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big(1-\frac{1}{4}\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\eta\big)\,\big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t+1}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}+5\eta\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d}}\,\big\|\bm{U}^{t,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\big(1-\frac{1}{4}\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\eta\big)\left(C_{7}\rho^{t}\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{local}}+C_{8}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}\right)\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+5\eta\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}r\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{d}}\left(2C_{1}\rho^{t}\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{local}}+2C_{2}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}\right)\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\big(1-\frac{1}{4}\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\eta\big)C_{7}+o\left(1\right)C_{1}\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\eta\right)\rho^{t}\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{local}}\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\big(1-\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\eta\big)C_{8}+o\left(1\right)C_{2}\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\eta\right)\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left(C_{7}\rho^{t+1}\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{local}}+C_{8}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}\right)\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty},

provided that
0<\eta\leq\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}/\big(32\lambda_{\max}^{\star 8/3}\big)
,
1-\lambda_{\min}^{\star 4/3}\eta/5\leq\rho<1
,
C_{7},C_{8}
are sufficiently large.

Recognizing that the above bound holds for any
1\leq m\leq d
, we conclude the proof.

A.6
Proof of Lemma
5.6

It is easy to see that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t+1}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}&\leq\big\|\bm{U}^{t+1}-\bm{U}^{t+1,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t+1,\left(m\right)}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}.\end{split}

(146)

Combining Lemma
5.4
and Lemma
5.5
, we conclude that with probability at least
1-O(d^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\big\|\big(\bm{U}^{t+1}-\bm{U}^{\star}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(C_{5}\rho^{t}\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{local}}+C_{6}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}\right)\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\left(C_{7}\rho^{t}\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{local}}+C_{8}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}\right)\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left(C_{3}\rho^{t}\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{local}}+C_{4}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}\right)\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty},

with the proviso that
C_{3}/\left(C_{5}+C_{7}\right)
and
C_{4}/\left(C_{6}+C_{8}\right)
are both sufficiently large.

Appendix B
Proofs for retrieving tensor components

B.1
Proof of Lemma
5.12

We shall often operate upon the event where the claims in Lemma
5.7

hold, which happens with very high probability (i.e. at least
1-O\left(d^{-10}\right)
).
Recall the definition of
\bm{\gamma}^{\star\tau}
in (
72
).
Since
\bm{\theta}^{\tau}=\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{g}^{\tau}
, this
allows us to write that: for each
1\leq i\leq r
,

\displaystyle\gamma_{i}^{\star\tau}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{i}^{\star}\left\langle\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star},\bm{g}^{\tau}\right\rangle,

where we recall that
\lambda_{i}^{\star}=\|\bm{u}_{i}^{\star}\|_{2}^{3}

and
\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star}=\bm{u}_{i}^{\star}/\|\bm{u}_{i}^{\star}\|_{2}
.
Given that
\bm{g}^{\tau}
is a Gaussian vector independent of
\bm{U}
,
we observe that
\bm{\gamma}^{\star\tau}
is zero-mean Gaussian conditional
on
\Omega
and
\bm{E}
. In order to understand the order statistics
associated with this vector, we first look at its covariance matrix.

Denote by
\bm{\Sigma}^{\tau}
the covariance matrix of
\bm{\gamma}^{\star\tau}

(conditional on
\bm{U}
). Then we have

\displaystyle\Sigma_{i,i}^{\tau}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{i}^{\star 2}\left\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\lambda_{i}^{\star 2}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}}\big(\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star}\big)\right\|_{2}^{2},

(147a)

\displaystyle\Sigma_{i,j}^{\tau}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{i}^{\star}\lambda_{j}^{\star}\left\langle\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star},\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\overline{\bm{u}}_{j}^{\star}\right\rangle=\lambda_{i}^{\star}\lambda_{j}^{\star}\left\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star},\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}}\big(\overline{\bm{u}}_{j}^{\star}\big)\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\lambda_{i}^{\star}\lambda_{j}^{\star}\Big(\big\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{j}^{\star}\big\rangle-\big\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star},\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}^{\perp}}\big(\overline{\bm{u}}_{j}^{\star}\big)\big\rangle\Big),

(147b)

where we denote by
\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}}(\bm{z})=\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{z}

and
\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}^{\perp}}(\bm{z})=\left(\bm{I}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\right)\bm{z}
.
In addition, since the unit vector
\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star}

lies in the span of the columns of
\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star}

(cf. (
54
)), it follows from Lemma
D.6

that

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}}\big(\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star}\big)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\big\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star}\big\|_{2}=\big\|\bm{U}\bm{R}\left(\bm{U}\bm{R}\right)^{\top}\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star}\big\|_{2}\geq\sqrt{1-\left\|\bm{U}\bm{R}-\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star}\right\|^{2}},

(148a)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}^{\perp}}\big(\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star}\big)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\big\|\big(\bm{I}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big)\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star}\big\|_{2}=\big\|\big(\bm{I}-\bm{U}\bm{R}\left(\bm{U}\bm{R}\right)^{\top}\big)\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star}\big\|_{2}\leq\left\|\bm{U}\bm{R}-\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star}\right\|,

(148b)

where
\bm{R}
is a rotation matrix defined in
(
55
). This together with (
147a
)
gives

\displaystyle\lambda_{i}^{\star 2}\big(1-\left\|\bm{U}\bm{R}-\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star}\right\|^{2}\big)\leq\Sigma_{i,i}^{\tau}\leq\lambda_{i}^{\star 2},

(149)

where we have also used the fact that
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}}\big(\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star}\big)\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star}\right\|_{2}=1
.
Moreover, taking together (
147a
), (
148b
)
and the incoherence condition, we see that for any
1\leq i\neq j\leq r
,

\displaystyle\left|\Sigma_{i,j}^{\tau}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{i}^{\star}\lambda_{j}^{\star}\left\{\left|\left\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{j}^{\star}\right\rangle\right|+\left\|\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\star}\right\|_{2}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}^{\perp}}\big(\overline{\bm{u}}_{j}^{\star}\big)\right\|_{2}\right\}\leq\lambda_{i}^{\star}\lambda_{j}^{\star}\underbrace{\big(\sqrt{\mu/d}+\left\|\bm{U}\bm{R}-\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star}\right\|\big)}_{=:\delta_{1}},

(150)

which is expected to be small if
\delta_{1}
is small.

From our assumptions on the sample size, the rank and the condition
number, we can invoke Lemma
5.7
to see that

\kappa r\delta_{1}\ll 1
, where
\delta_{1}
is defined in (
150
)
and
\kappa=\lambda_{\max}^{\star}/\lambda_{\min}^{\star}
. Thus,
we can decompose
\bm{\Sigma}^{\tau}
into two components as follows

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}^{\tau}=\underbrace{\left(1-\kappa r\delta_{1}\right)\bm{D}^{\star 2}}_{=:\,\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\tau}}+\underbrace{\bm{\Sigma}^{\tau}-\left(1-\kappa r\delta_{1}\right)\bm{D}^{\star 2}}_{=:\,\breve{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\tau}},

where

\displaystyle\bm{D}^{\star}:=\mathsf{diag}\left(\lambda_{1}^{\star},\cdots,\lambda_{r}^{\star}\right)\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}.

As it turns out, both
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\tau}
and
\breve{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\tau}

are positive definite. Indeed, we first learn from (
149
)
and (
150
) that: the
i
-th digaonal entry of
\breve{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\tau}

obeys

\displaystyle\breve{\Sigma}_{i,i}^{\tau}

\displaystyle\geq\lambda_{i}^{\star 2}\big(1-\left\|\bm{U}\bm{R}-\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star}\right\|^{2}\big)-\left(1-\kappa r\delta_{1}\right)\lambda_{i}^{\star 2}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{i}^{\star 2}\big(\kappa r\delta_{1}-\left\|\bm{U}\bm{R}-\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star}\right\|^{2}\big)\geq\lambda_{i}^{\star 2}\left(\kappa r\delta_{1}-\delta_{1}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\text{i})}{>}\kappa\lambda_{i}^{\star 2}\left(r-1\right)\delta_{1}\overset{\text{(ii)}}{\geq}\lambda_{i}^{\star}\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\left(r-1\right)\delta_{1}

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{j:j\neq i}\big|\breve{\Sigma}_{i,j}^{\tau}\big|,

where (i) holds since
\delta_{1}<1
under our assumptions, (ii)
follows since
\kappa\lambda_{i}^{\star}\geq\kappa\lambda_{\min}^{\star}=\lambda_{\max}^{\star}
,
and the last line makes use of (
150
). This implies that

\breve{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\tau}
is diagonally dominant, and hence
\breve{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\tau}\succeq\bm{0}
.
In conclusion, both
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\tau}
and
\breve{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\tau}

are positive definite.

Let
\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}^{\star\tau}
and
\breve{\bm{\gamma}}^{\star\tau}

be independent zero-mean Gaussian random vectors with covariance matrices

\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\tau}
and
\breve{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\tau}
,
respectively. Clearly, the distribution of
\bm{\gamma}^{\star\tau}

is identical to that of
\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}^{\star\tau}+\breve{\bm{\gamma}}^{\star\tau}
.
Consequently, it allows us to look at the distributions of these two
random vectors separately.

•

In view of (
149
) and the fact
\kappa r\delta_{1}<1
, one
has

\displaystyle\breve{\Sigma}_{i,i}^{\tau}\leq\lambda_{i}^{\star 2}-\left(1-\kappa r\delta_{1}\right)\lambda_{i}^{\star 2}=\kappa r\delta_{1}\lambda_{i}^{\star 2}.

(151)

Thus, with probability at least

1-O\left(d^{-10}\right)
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\breve{\bm{\gamma}}^{\star\tau}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}\Big[\max_{1\leq i\leq r}\left|\breve{\gamma}_{i}^{\star\tau}\right|\Big]+\max_{1\leq i\leq r}\sqrt{\mathsf{Var}\big(\breve{\gamma}_{i}^{\star\tau}\big)\log d}\lesssim\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\sqrt{\kappa r\delta_{1}\log d}\ll\lambda_{\min}^{\star}/\big(r\sqrt{\log d}\big),

(152)

where the last step arises from
\kappa^{3}r^{3}\delta_{1}\log^{2}d\ll 1
under our sample size, noise and rank conditions. By the condition that
L\asymp r^{2\kappa^{2}}\log^{3/2}r
,
r\ll d
and
\kappa\asymp 1
, we take a union bound over
\tau\in\left[L\right]
to find that each entry of
\breve{\bm{\gamma}}^{\star\tau}
is fairly small for all
\tau\in\left[L\right]
. Another immediate consequence
of (
152
) is that: for any fixed vector
\bm{v}\in\mathbb{R}^{r}
, with probability
at least
1-O(d^{-10})
, for all
\tau\in\left[L\right]
,

\displaystyle\left\|\breve{\bm{\gamma}}^{\star\tau}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r}\left\|\breve{\bm{\gamma}}^{\star\tau}\right\|_{\infty}\ll\lambda_{\min}^{\star},

(153a)

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\bm{v},\breve{\bm{\gamma}}^{\star\tau}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{v}\right\|_{2}\left\|\breve{\bm{\gamma}}^{\star\tau}\right\|_{2}\ll\left\|\bm{v}\right\|_{2}\lambda_{\min}^{\star}.

(153b)

•

We then turn attention to
\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}^{\star\tau}
, which
is composed of independent Gaussian random variables. Let us define
\widehat{\Delta}^{\tau}:=\widehat{\gamma}_{1}^{\star\tau}-\max_{1<i\leq r}\big|\widehat{\gamma}_{i}^{\star\tau}\big|
for each
1\leq\tau\leq L
and let
\widehat{\Delta}_{1}^{\left(1\right)}\geq\widehat{\Delta}_{1}^{\left(2\right)}\geq\dots\geq\widehat{\Delta}_{1}^{\left(L\right)}
denote the order statistics of
\big\{\widehat{\Delta}_{1}^{\tau}\big\}_{\tau=1}^{L}
in descending order. Fix any small
constant
\delta>0
. Invoke Lemma
D.5

to demonstrate that: with probability greater than
1-\delta/r
,

\displaystyle\widehat{\Delta}_{1}^{\left(1\right)}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\lambda_{\min}^{\star},

(154a)

\displaystyle\widehat{\Delta}_{1}^{\left(1\right)}-\widehat{\Delta}_{1}^{\left(2\right)}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}{r\sqrt{\log d}},

(154b)

where we use the conditions that
L\asymp r^{2\kappa^{2}}\log^{3/2}r
,
r\ll d
and
\kappa\asymp 1
.
In addition, let
\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}_{\smallsetminus 1}^{\star\tau}:=\big[\widehat{\gamma}_{2}^{\star\tau},\cdots,\widehat{\gamma}_{r}^{\star\tau}\big]^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{r-1}
.
We know from standard Gaussian concentration inequalities and union bounds that for any fixed vector
\bm{v}\in\mathbb{R}^{r}
, with
probability
1-O(d^{-20})
, for all
\tau\in\left[L\right]
,

\displaystyle\widehat{\gamma}_{1}^{\star\tau}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big(\sqrt{\log L}+\sqrt{\log d}\big)\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\asymp\sqrt{\log d}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star},

(155a)

\displaystyle\big\|\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\widehat{\gamma}_{1}^{\star\tau}+\big\|\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}_{\smallsetminus 1}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{2}\lesssim\big(\sqrt{\log d}+\sqrt{r\log d}\big)\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\lesssim\sqrt{r\log d}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star},

(155b)

\displaystyle\big|\big\langle\bm{v},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}^{\star\tau}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\leq\widehat{\gamma}_{1}^{\star\tau}\left\|\bm{v}\right\|_{2}+\big|\big\langle\bm{v}_{\smallsetminus 1},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}_{\smallsetminus 1}^{\star\tau}\big\rangle\big|\lesssim\left\|\bm{v}\right\|_{2}\sqrt{\log d}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star},

(155c)

where
\bm{v}_{\smallsetminus 1}:=\left[v_{2},\cdots,\cdots,v_{r}\right]\in\mathbb{R}^{r-1}
.

•

Putting (
152
) and (
154
)
together and invoking the triangle inequality immediately establish (
74a
) and (
74b
).
On the other hand, combining (
152
) with (
155a
) proves (
75a
); (
153a
) and (
155b
)
taken collectively establish (
75b
),
whereas (
153b
) and (
155c
)
prove (
75c
).

B.2
Proof of Lemma
5.13

Recall that the vector of interest
\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau}
is the
leading singular vector of
\bm{M}^{\tau}
(as constructed in (
70c
)),
where
\bm{M}^{\tau}
satisfies

\displaystyle\bm{M}^{\tau}

\displaystyle=p^{-1}\bm{T}\times_{3}\bm{\theta}^{\tau}=\bm{T}^{\star}\times_{3}\bm{\theta}^{\tau}+\left(p^{-1}\bm{T}-\bm{T}^{\star}\right)\times_{3}\bm{\theta}^{\tau}

\displaystyle=\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star\top}+\sum_{s:s\neq 1}\gamma_{s}^{\star\tau}\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star\top}+\left(p^{-1}\bm{T}-\bm{T}^{\star}\right)\times_{3}\bm{\theta}^{\tau}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star\top}+\sum_{s:s\neq 1}\gamma_{s}^{\star\tau}\left(\bm{I}-\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star\top}\right)\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star\top}\left(\bm{I}-\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star\top}\right)}_{=:\,\bm{M}^{\star\tau}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\underbrace{\sum_{s:s\neq 1}\gamma_{s}^{\star\tau}\left\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\rangle\left(\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star\top}+\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star\top}\right)-\sum_{s:s\neq 1}\gamma_{s}^{\star\tau}\left\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\rangle^{2}\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star\top}}_{=:\,\bm{C}^{\tau}}+\underbrace{\left(p^{-1}\bm{T}-\bm{T}^{\star}\right)\times_{3}\bm{\theta}^{\tau}}_{=:\,\bm{F}^{\tau}},

(156)

and
\gamma_{i}^{\star\tau}\penalty\ (1\leq i\leq r)
is defined in (
72
).

In what follows, we shall view
\bm{C}^{\tau}
and
\bm{F}^{\tau}
as perturbation terms
superimposed on
\bm{M}^{\star\tau}
. Lemma
B.1
below proves that their operator
norms are all small under our sample size, noise and rank conditions, which enables to apply
Wedin’s theorem to justify the
\ell_{2}
proximity between
\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau}

and
\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}
.

Lemma B.1
.

Instate the assumptions of Lemma
5.13
. With probability at least
1-O\left(d^{-10}\right)
, one has

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{F}^{\tau}\big\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\mu r}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\log^{3}d}{d^{3/2}p}+\frac{\mu r\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\log^{5/2}d}{d\sqrt{p}}+\frac{\sigma\log^{7/2}d}{p}+\sigma\sqrt{\frac{rd\log^{5}d}{p}},

(157)

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{C}^{\tau}\big\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d}}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star},

(158)

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{F}^{\tau}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu r\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\log d}{d\sqrt{p}}+\sigma\sqrt{\frac{rd\log^{2}d}{p}},

(159)

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{C}^{\tau}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d}}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}.

(160)

Proof.

See Appendix
B.3
.∎

As a consequence, recalling the definition of
\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{op}}
and
\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{proj}}
in (
81
) and (
79
) respectively, one has

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{M}^{\tau}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\bm{F}^{\tau}\big\|+\big\|\bm{C}^{\tau}\big\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\underbrace{\frac{\sqrt{\mu r}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\log^{3}d}{d^{3/2}p}+\frac{\mu r\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\log^{5/2}d}{d\sqrt{p}}+\frac{\sigma\log^{7/2}d}{p}+\sigma\sqrt{\frac{rd\log^{5}d}{p}}+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d}}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}}_{=\,\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{op}}\cdot\lambda_{\min}^{\star}},

(161)

and

\displaystyle\big\|\big(\bm{M}^{\tau}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}\leq\big\|\bm{F}^{\tau}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}+\big\|\bm{C}^{\tau}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\underbrace{\frac{\mu r\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\log d}{d\sqrt{p}}+\sigma\sqrt{\frac{rd\log^{2}d}{p}}+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d}}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}}_{=\,\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{proj}}\cdot\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}.

(162)

It then follows from Weyl’s inequality that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in\left[d\right]}

\displaystyle\left|\sigma_{i}\big(\bm{M}^{\tau}\big)-\sigma_{i}\big(\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)\right|\leq\big\|\bm{M}^{\tau}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big\|\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{op}}\cdot\lambda_{\min}^{\star}\ll\lambda_{\min}^{\star}

(163)

where
\sigma_{i}(\bm{Z})
denotes the
i
-th largest singular value
of a matrix
\bm{Z}
and we use the condition that
\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{op}}\ll 1
. All in all, these arguments justify that the
spectrum of
\bm{M}^{\tau}
is fairly close to that of
\bm{M}^{\star\tau}
.

Next, we look at the gap between the two leading singular
values of
\bm{M}^{\star\tau}
. To begin with, it is self-evident from
the definition of
\bm{M}^{\star\tau}
that:
\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}

is the singular vector of
\bm{M}^{\star\tau}
. In fact, we claim
one further result, that is,
\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}
is indeed
the leading singular vector of
\bm{M}^{\star\tau}
whose singular value
is given by
\sigma_{1}\big(\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)=\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}
.
Towards this end, let us define

\displaystyle\bm{U}_{\smallsetminus 1}^{\tau}:=\left(\bm{I}-\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star\top}\right)\overline{\bm{U}}^{\star}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times\left(r-1\right)}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\bm{\gamma}_{\smallsetminus 1}^{\star\tau}:=\big[\gamma_{2}^{\star\tau},\cdots,\gamma_{r}^{\star\tau}\big]^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{r-1},

allowing us to write

\displaystyle\sum_{s:s\neq 1}\gamma_{s}^{\star\tau}\left(\bm{I}-\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star\top}\right)\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star\top}\left(\bm{I}-\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star\top}\right)=\bm{U}_{\smallsetminus 1}^{\tau}\mathsf{diag}\big(\bm{\gamma}_{\smallsetminus 1}^{\star\tau}\big)\bm{U}_{\smallsetminus 1}^{\tau\top}=:\,\bm{M}_{\smallsetminus 1}^{\star\tau}.

We note that from Lemma
D.1
, one has

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{U}_{\smallsetminus 1}^{\tau}\big\|=\big\|\left(\bm{I}-\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star\top}\right)\overline{\bm{U}}^{\star}\big\|\leq\big\|\overline{\bm{U}}^{\star}\big\|\leq\sqrt{1+r\sqrt{\mu/d}}.

Let
|\gamma^{\star\tau}|_{(1)}\geq\dots\geq|\gamma^{\star\tau}|_{(r)}
denote the absolute values of
\{\gamma_{i}^{\star\tau}\}_{i=1}^{r}
in descending order. This together with Lemma
5.12
implies that

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{M}_{\smallsetminus 1}^{\star\tau}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big|\gamma^{\star\tau}\big|_{(2)}\big\|\bm{U}_{\smallsetminus 1}^{\tau}\big\|^{2}\leq\big|\gamma^{\star\tau}\big|_{(2)}\big(1+r\sqrt{\mu/d}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}-\big(\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}-\big|\gamma^{\star\tau}\big|_{(2)}\big)+r\sqrt{\mu/d}\,\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}<\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau},

as long as
\kappa r\sqrt{(\mu\log d)/d}\ll 1
. Given that
\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}
is the
singular vector of
\bm{M}^{\star\tau}
with singular value
\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}
,
we can conclude that
\sigma_{1}\big(\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)=\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}
.
This also allows us to lower bound the gap between the two largest
singular values
\bm{M}^{\star\tau}
as follows

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}\big(\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)-\sigma_{2}\big(\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)

\displaystyle\geq\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}-\big\|\bm{M}_{\smallsetminus 1}^{\star\tau}\big\|\geq\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}-\big|\gamma^{\star\tau}\big|_{(2)}\big(1+r\sqrt{\mu/d}\big)

\displaystyle\gtrsim\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}-\big|\gamma^{\star\tau}\big|_{(2)}\gtrsim\lambda_{\min}^{\star},

(164)

provided that
\kappa r\sqrt{(\mu\log d)/d}\ll 1
. We also know from (
163
) and (
164
) that

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}\big(\bm{M}^{\tau}\big)-\sigma_{2}\big(\bm{M}^{\tau}\big)\geq\sigma_{1}\big(\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)-\sigma_{2}\big(\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)-2\,\big\|\bm{M}^{\tau}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big\|\gtrsim\lambda_{\min}^{\star}.

Combined with (
162
) and Wedin’s theorem, we conclude that

\displaystyle\big\|\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau}-\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\big\|\big(\bm{M}^{\tau}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}}{\sigma_{1}\big(\bm{M}^{\tau}\big)-\sigma_{2}\big(\bm{M}^{\tau}\big)-\big\|\bm{M}^{\tau}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big\|}\lesssim\frac{\mu r\log d}{d\sqrt{p}}+\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{rd\log^{2}d}{p}}+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d}}.

(165)

Here, we have made use of the fact that
\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau}

is the leading singular vector of
\bm{M}^{\tau}

obeying
\big\langle\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\rangle\geq 0
.

B.3
Proof of Lemma
B.1

B.3.1
Controlling
\bm{F}^{\tau}

•

We first consider the spectral norm of
\bm{F}^{\tau}
. Recall the definition
that
\bm{\theta}^{\tau}=\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{g}^{\tau}
. Let us define

\bm{\theta}^{\star\tau}:=\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star\top}\,\bm{g}^{\tau}

and decompose

\displaystyle\bm{F}^{\tau}=\left(p^{-1}\bm{T}-\bm{T}^{\star}\right)\times_{3}\bm{\theta}^{\tau}=\underbrace{\left(p^{-1}\bm{T}-\bm{T}^{\star}\right)\times_{3}\bm{\theta}^{\star\tau}}_{=:\,\bm{X}}+\underbrace{\left(p^{-1}\bm{T}-\bm{T}^{\star}\right)\times_{3}\big(\bm{\theta}^{\tau}-\bm{\theta}^{\star\tau}\big)}_{=:\,\bm{Y}}.

In the sequel, we shall control these two terms separately.

–

To bound

\left\|\bm{X}\right\|
, observe that
\bm{\theta}^{\star\tau}

is independent of
p^{-1}\bm{T}-\bm{T}^{\star}
. By Lemma
D.4
,
one has

\big\|\left(p^{-1}\bm{T}-\bm{T}^{\star}\right)\times_{3}\bm{\theta}^{\star\tau}\big\|\lesssim\big\|\bm{\theta}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{dp}}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\big\|\bm{\theta}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{\infty}\frac{\sigma\log^{5/2}d}{p}+\big\|\bm{\theta}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{2}\sigma\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}.

(166)

This suggests that we need to control the
\ell_{\infty}

and
\ell_{2}
norms of
\bm{\theta}^{\star\tau}
.
Using standard results on Gaussian random vectors and Lemma
D.1
, we know that with
probability at least
1-O\left(d^{-20}\right)
,

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{\theta}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\big\|\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star\top}\,\bm{g}^{\tau}\big\|_{\infty}\lesssim\left\|\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}\sqrt{\log d}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d}},

(167)

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{\theta}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\big\|\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star\top}\,\bm{g}^{\tau}\big\|_{2}\lesssim\left\|\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\sqrt{\log d}=\sqrt{r\log d}.

(168)

Combining (
166
) with (
167
) and
(
168
) reveals that with
probability exceeding
1-O\left(d^{-20}\right)
,

\displaystyle\big\|\left(p^{-1}\bm{T}-\bm{T}^{\star}\right)\times_{3}\bm{\theta}^{\star\tau}\big\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu r\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\log d}{d\sqrt{p}}+\frac{\sigma}{p}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log^{6}d}{d}}+\sigma\sqrt{\frac{rd\log^{2}d}{p}}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\mu r\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\log d}{d\sqrt{p}}+\sigma\sqrt{\frac{rd\log^{2}d}{p}},

(169)

where the last inequality holds as long as
p\gtrsim\mu d^{-2}\log^{4}d
.

–

Turning to
\bm{Y}
, we can simply upper bound

\displaystyle\big\|\left(p^{-1}\bm{T}-\bm{T}^{\star}\right)\times_{3}\big(\bm{\theta}^{\tau}-\bm{\theta}^{\star\tau}\big)\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|p^{-1}\bm{T}-\bm{T}^{\star}\right\|\big\|\bm{\theta}^{\tau}-\bm{\theta}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{2}.

Since
\mathsf{rank}\left(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star\top}\right)\leq 2r
,
Lemma
5.7
and the standard result of Gaussian random vectors yields
that: with probability at least
1-O\left(d^{-12}\right)
,

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{\theta}^{\tau}-\bm{\theta}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\left(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star\top}\right)\bm{g}^{\tau}\right\|_{2}\lesssim\left\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star\top}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\sqrt{\log d}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{\mathsf{orth}}^{\star\top}\right\|\sqrt{2r\log d}\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{se}}\sqrt{r\log d}\ll 1,

(170)

where we recall the definition of
\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{se}}
in (
57
) and that
\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{se}}\ll 1/\sqrt{r\log d}
by our conditions. Moreover, by Lemma
D.2
, we know
that with probability exceeding
1-O\left(d^{-10}\right)
,

\displaystyle\left\|p^{-1}\bm{T}-\bm{T}^{\star}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|p^{-1}\mathcal{P}_{\Omega}\left(\bm{T}^{\star}\right)-\bm{T}^{\star}\right\|+\left\|p^{-1}\mathcal{P}_{\Omega}\left(\bm{E}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\mu r}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\log^{3}d}{d^{3/2}p}+\frac{\mu\sqrt{r}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\log^{5/2}d}{d\sqrt{p}}+\frac{\sigma\log^{7/2}d}{p}+\sigma\sqrt{\frac{d\log^{5}d}{p}}.

(171)

Combining (
170
) and (
171
), we find that

\displaystyle\big\|\left(p^{-1}\bm{T}-\bm{T}^{\star}\right)\times_{3}\big(\bm{\theta}^{\tau}-\bm{\theta}^{\star\tau}\big)\big\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\mu r}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\log^{3}d}{d^{3/2}p}+\frac{\mu\sqrt{r}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\log^{5/2}d}{d\sqrt{p}}+\frac{\sigma\log^{7/2}d}{p}+\sigma\sqrt{\frac{d\log^{5}d}{p}}.

(172)

Putting (
169
) and (
172
)
together shows that

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{F}^{\tau}\big\|\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\mu r}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\log^{3}d}{d^{3/2}p}+\frac{\mu r\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\log^{5/2}d}{d\sqrt{p}}+\frac{\sigma\log^{7/2}d}{p}+\sigma\sqrt{\frac{rd\log^{5}d}{p}}.

•

Next, we turn to
\big\|\bm{F}^{\tau}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}
.
By the definition of the operator norm, we know that

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{F}^{\tau}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\left(p^{-1}\bm{T}-\bm{T}^{\star}\right)\times_{2}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\times_{3}\bm{\theta}^{\tau}\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\left(p^{-1}\bm{T}-\bm{T}^{\star}\right)\times_{2}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\|\big\|\bm{\theta}^{\tau}\big\|_{2}.

Applying Lemma
D.4
again reveals that
with probability at least
1-O\left(d^{-12}\right)
,

\displaystyle\left\|\left(p^{-1}\bm{T}-\bm{T}^{\star}\right)\times_{2}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{dp}}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\left\|\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\|_{\infty}\frac{\sigma\log^{5/2}d}{p}+\left\|\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\|_{2}\sigma\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu\sqrt{r}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\sqrt{\log d}}{d\sqrt{p}}+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{\mu\log^{5}d}}{\sqrt{d}\,p}+\sigma\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\mu\sqrt{r}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\sqrt{\log d}}{d\sqrt{p}}+\sigma\sqrt{\frac{d\log d}{p}},

(173)

where the last step arises from the condition that
p\gtrsim\mu d^{-2}\log^{4}d
.
In addition, realizing that
\bm{U}
consists of eigenvectors, standard Gaussian random vectors results
give that with probability at least
1-O\left(d^{-12}\right)
,

\left\|\bm{\theta}^{\tau}\right\|_{2}=\big\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{g}^{\tau}\big\|_{2}\lesssim\left\|\bm{U}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\sqrt{\log d}=\sqrt{r\log d}.

(174)

Combining (
173
) and (
174
) shows that with probability exceeding
1-O\left(d^{-10}\right)
,

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{F}^{\tau}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}\lesssim\frac{\mu r\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\log d}{d\sqrt{p}}+\sigma\sqrt{\frac{rd\log^{2}d}{p}}.

B.3.2
Controlling
\bm{C}^{\tau}

Recall the definition of
\bm{C}^{\tau}
in (
156
). We first consider
the spectral norm of
\bm{C}^{\tau}
. It is straightforward to compute
that

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{C}^{\tau}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\,\Big\|\sum\nolimits_{s:s\neq 1}\gamma_{s}^{\star\tau}\left\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\rangle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}\Big\|_{2}+\Big|\sum\nolimits_{s:s\neq 1}\gamma_{s}^{\star\tau}\left\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\rangle^{2}\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{s:s\neq 1}\left|\left\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\rangle\right|\big\|\overline{\bm{U}}^{\star}\big\|\big\|\bm{\gamma}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{2}+\max_{s:s\neq 1}\left\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\rangle^{2}\sqrt{r}\big\|\bm{\gamma}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Big(\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{d}}+\frac{\mu\sqrt{r}}{d}\Big)\sqrt{r\log d}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\asymp\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d}}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}

(175)

if
\mu r/d\lesssim 1
, where we recall that
\overline{\bm{U}}^{\star}=\left[\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star},\cdots,\overline{\bm{u}}_{r}^{\star}\right]
.
Here, the last line holds owing to (
8c
),
Lemma
D.1
(which justifies that
\big\|\overline{\bm{U}}^{\star}\big\|\lesssim 1

if
r\sqrt{\mu/d}\leq 1
) and Lemma
5.12
.

The claim (
160
) arises from the definition of the spectral norm that
\big\|\bm{C}^{\tau}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}\leq\big\|\bm{C}^{\tau}\big\|
.

B.4
Proof of Lemma
5.14

Let us fix an arbitrary
m\in\left[d\right]
. We remind the readers of several definitions:
(1)
\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}
: see (
72
);
(2)
\bm{M}^{\star\tau}
: see (
156
); and (3)
\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}
: see (
70d
).

Before continuing, we state two immediate facts. First, it has already
been observed in Appendix
B.2
that
\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}

is a singular vector of
\bm{M}^{\star\tau}
with singular value
\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}
,
and hence

\displaystyle\big(\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big)_{m}

\displaystyle=\big(\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}\big)^{-1}\bm{M}_{m,:}^{\star\tau}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}.

(176)

Here and throughout,
\bm{A}_{m,:}
denotes the
m
-th row of a
matrix
\bm{A}
. Second,
\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}

is the top singular vector of
\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}

such that
\langle\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\rangle\geq 0
,
and we denote by
\gamma_{\tau}^{(m)}
the associated singular value.
Recall our definition of
\bm{\nu}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}
in Algorithm
6
. Similar to the case of
\bm{\nu}^{\tau}
, we will show shortly in Lemma
5.16
that the global signs of
\bm{\nu}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}
and
\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}
coincide, and hence

\displaystyle\bm{\nu}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}=\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}.

(177)

As a result, the proof of this lemma boils down to showing that
\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}
(and hence
\bm{\nu}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}
)
is sufficiently close to

\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}
in the
m
-th entry.
Towards this end, observe that

\displaystyle\big(\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}\big)_{m}

\displaystyle=\big(\gamma_{\tau}^{(m)}\big)^{-1}\bm{M}_{m,:}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}.

(178)

The above two facts (
176
) and (
178
)
together with the triangle inequality lead to

\displaystyle\Big|\big(\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big)_{m}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\Big\{\big(\gamma_{\tau}^{(m)}\big)^{-1}-\big(\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}\big)^{-1}\Big\}\bm{M}_{m,:}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\big(\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}\big)^{-1}\left|\big(\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)_{m,:}\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\big(\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}\big)^{-1}\left|\bm{M}_{m,:}^{\star\tau}\big(\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left|\big(\gamma_{\tau}^{(m)}\big)^{-1}-\big(\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}\big)^{-1}\right|\big\|\bm{M}_{m,:}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}}_{=:\,\alpha_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\big(\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}\big)^{-1}\big\|\big(\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)_{m,:}\big\|_{2}\big\|\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}}_{=:\,\alpha_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\big(\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}\big)^{-1}\big\|\bm{M}_{m,:}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{2}\big\|\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}}_{=:\,\alpha_{3}}.

(179)

Therefore, it suffices to upper bound the above three quantities separately.

B.4.1
Controlling
\alpha_{3}

The first step to bound
\alpha_{3}
(cf. (
179
))
is to control
\big\|\bm{M}_{m,:}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{2}
. Towards this
end, we first observe from the incoherence conditions that

\displaystyle\max_{s:s\neq 1}\big|\left(\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}-\left\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\rangle\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right)_{m}\big|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{s:s\neq 1}\left\|\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}\right\|_{\infty}+\max_{s:s\neq 1}\big|\left\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\rangle\big|\left\|\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{s:s\neq 1}\left\|\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}\right\|_{\infty}+\max_{s:s\neq 1}\left\|\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}\right\|_{2}\left\|\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\|_{2}\left\|\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\|_{\infty}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{d}}.

(180)

When combined with the definition
(
156
), this gives

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{M}_{m,:}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Big\|\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}\left(\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right)_{m}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star\top}+\sum\nolimits_{s:s\neq 1}\gamma_{s}^{\star\tau}\left(\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}-\left\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\rangle\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right)_{m}\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star\top}\left(\bm{I}-\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star\top}\right)\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}\left\|\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\|_{\infty}\left\|\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\|_{2}+\Big\|\sum\nolimits_{s:s\neq 1}\gamma_{s}^{\star\tau}\left(\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}-\left\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\rangle\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right)_{m}\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star\top}\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}\left\|\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\|_{\infty}+\max_{s:s\neq 1}\big|\left(\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}-\left\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\rangle\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right)_{m}\big|\big\|\bm{\gamma}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{2}\big\|\overline{\bm{U}}^{\star}\big\|

\displaystyle\overset{(\text{i})}{\lesssim}\gamma_{1}^{\star\tau}\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{d}}+\big\|\bm{\gamma}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{d}}\asymp\big\|\bm{\gamma}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{d}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d}}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star},

(181)

where (i) arises from (
180
) and
\big\|\overline{\bm{U}}^{\star}\big\|\lesssim 1

if
r\sqrt{\mu/d}\ll 1
, and the last step comes from Lemma
5.12
.

The second step is to upper bound
\big\|\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}
.
Towards this, we resort to Wedin’s theorem as follows

\displaystyle\big\|\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}\leq\frac{\big\|\big(\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}}{\sigma_{1}\big(\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)-\sigma_{2}\big(\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)-\big\|\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big\|},

(182)

where we rely on the fact that
\big\langle\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\rangle\geq 0
. To complete this bound, we need
to control
\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}
. Before we
move on, we find it helpful to introduce

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{M}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}:=p^{-1}\bm{T}\times_{3}\bm{\theta}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}.

(183)

Let
\widehat{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}
denote the top left singular
vector of
\widehat{\bm{M}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}
such that

\displaystyle\big\langle\widehat{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\rangle\geq 0.

(184)

Since we have already bounded
\big\|\bm{M}^{\tau}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big\|

in Lemma
B.1
, we can decompose

\displaystyle\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}=\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{M}^{\tau}+\bm{M}^{\tau}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}=\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\widehat{\bm{M}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}+\widehat{\bm{M}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{M}^{\tau}+\bm{M}^{\tau}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}.

With these definitions in place, Lemma
B.2

below provides the desired bounds.

Lemma B.2
.

Instate the assumptions
of Lemma
5.14
. With probability at least
1-O\left(d^{-10}\right)
, the following
holds simultaneously for all
1\leq m\leq d
:

\displaystyle\big\|\widehat{\bm{M}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}\big\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu r\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\sqrt{\log d}}{d\sqrt{p}}+\sigma\sqrt{\frac{rd\log d}{p}},

(185)

\displaystyle\big\|\widehat{\bm{M}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{M}^{\tau}\big\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{loo}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d}}\lambda_{\max}^{\star},

(186)

where
\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{loo}}
is defined in (
65
).
As a result, one has

\displaystyle\big\|\big(\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}\leq\big\|\bm{M}^{\tau}-\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}\big\|\lesssim\frac{\mu r\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\sqrt{\log d}}{d\sqrt{p}}+\sigma\sqrt{\frac{rd\log d}{p}}.

(187)

Proof.

See Appendix
B.5
.∎

We then can further combine (
161
) and (
162
) to deduce that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big\|\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big\|&\leq\big\|\bm{M}^{\tau}-\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}\big\|+\big\|\bm{M}^{\tau}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big\|\\
&\lesssim\underbrace{\frac{\sqrt{\mu r}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\log^{3}d}{d^{3/2}p}+\frac{\mu r\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\log^{5/2}d}{d\sqrt{p}}+\frac{\sigma\log^{7/2}d}{p}+\sigma\sqrt{\frac{rd\log^{5}d}{p}}+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d}}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}}_{=\,\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{op}}\cdot\lambda_{\min}^{\star}},\end{split}

(188)

and

\displaystyle\big\|\big(\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}\lesssim\underbrace{\frac{\mu r\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\log d}{d\sqrt{p}}+\sigma\sqrt{\frac{rd\log^{2}d}{p}}+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d}}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}}_{=\,\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{proj}}\cdot\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}.

(189)

In particular,
we have
\big\|\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big\|\ll\lambda_{\min}^{\star}
under our conditions,
and it follows from (
164
) that

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}\big(\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)-\sigma_{2}\big(\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)-\big\|\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big\|\gtrsim\lambda_{\min}^{\star}.

Invoke the bound (
182
) to obtain

\displaystyle\big\|\overline{\bm{u}}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\big\|\big(\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\big\|_{2}\lesssim\underbrace{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d\sqrt{p}}+\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{\star}}\sqrt{\frac{rd\log^{2}d}{p}}+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d}}}_{=\,\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{proj}}}.

(190)

To finish up, combine this with (
181
) and the spectral
condition to arrive at

\displaystyle\alpha_{3}\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{proj}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d}}\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{op}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d}},

(191)

which results from the fact that
\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{proj}}\leq\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{op}}
(cf. (
81
)).

B.4.2
Controlling
\alpha_{2}

We then turn to
\alpha_{2}
(cf. (
179
)).
Recall the definition of
\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}
in (
70c
).
It is straightforward to verify that

\displaystyle\big(\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)_{m,:}

\displaystyle=\big(p^{-1}\bm{T}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}\times_{3}\bm{\theta}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{T}^{\star}\times_{3}\bm{\theta}^{\tau}\big)_{m,:}+\big(\bm{T}^{\star}\times_{3}\bm{\theta}^{\tau}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)_{m,:}

\displaystyle=\big(p^{-1}\bm{T}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}\times_{3}\bm{\theta}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{T}^{\star}\times_{3}\bm{\theta}^{\tau}\big)_{m,:}+\bm{C}_{m,:}^{\tau}

\displaystyle=\bm{T}_{m,:,:}^{\star}\times_{3}\big(\bm{\theta}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{\theta}^{\tau}\big)+\bm{C}_{m,:}^{\tau},

where
\bm{C}^{\tau}
is defined in (
156
).

From the incoherence conditions, we can upper bound

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{C}_{m,:}^{\tau}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Big\|\sum\nolimits_{s:s\neq 1}\gamma_{s}^{\star\tau}\left\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\rangle\left(\left(\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right)_{m}\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star\top}+\left(\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}\right)_{m}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star\top}\right)-\sum\nolimits_{s:s\neq 1}\gamma_{s}^{\star\tau}\left\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\rangle^{2}\left(\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right)_{m}\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star\top}\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\sum\nolimits_{s:s\neq 1}\gamma_{s}^{\star\tau}\left\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\rangle\left(\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right)_{m}\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star\top}\Big\|_{2}+\Big|\sum\nolimits_{s:s\neq 1}\gamma_{s}^{\star\tau}\left\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\rangle\left(\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}\right)_{m}\Big|+\Big|\sum\nolimits_{s:s\neq 1}\gamma_{s}^{\star\tau}\left\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\rangle^{2}\left(\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right)_{m}\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\bm{\gamma}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{2}\Big(\max_{s:s\neq 1}\left|\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\rangle\right|\left\|\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\|_{\infty}\big\|\overline{\bm{U}}^{\star}\big\|+\max_{s:s\neq 1}\left|\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\rangle\right|\big\|\overline{\bm{U}}^{\star}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\max_{s:s\neq 1}\left|\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\rangle\right|^{2}\left\|\overline{\bm{u}}_{1}^{\star}\right\|_{\infty}\sqrt{r}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\bm{\gamma}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{2}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{d}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{d}}+\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{d}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d}}+\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{d}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d}}\right)\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d}}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star},

(192)

where we use the fact that
\big\|\bm{\gamma}^{\star\tau}\big\|_{2}\lesssim\sqrt{r\log d}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}
from Lemma
5.12
and
\big\|\overline{\bm{U}}^{\star}\big\|\lesssim 1
from Lemma
D.1
.

In addition, we can express

\displaystyle\bm{T}_{m,:,:}^{\star}\times_{3}\big(\bm{\theta}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{\theta}^{\tau}\big)=\sum_{s\in\left[r\right]}\lambda_{s}^{\star}\big(\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}\big)_{m}\big\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\bm{\theta}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{\theta}^{\tau}\big\rangle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}.

By construction, we know that

\displaystyle\bm{\theta}^{\tau}-\bm{\theta}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}

\displaystyle=\big(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{U}^{(m)}\bm{U}^{\left(m\right)\top}\big)\,\bm{g}^{\tau}

is a zero-mean Gaussian random vector conditional on
\mathcal{P}_{\Omega}\left(\bm{E}\right)
.
Using standard results on Gaussian random vectors, one has: with
probability at least
1-O\left(d^{-11}\right)
, for each
s\in\left[r\right]
and
m\in\left[d\right]
,

\displaystyle\left|\big\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\bm{\theta}^{\tau}-\bm{\theta}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}\big\rangle\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\big\langle\bm{g}^{\tau},\big(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{U}^{(m)}\bm{U}^{\left(m\right)\top}\big)\,\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}\big\rangle\right|\lesssim\big\|\big(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{U}^{(m)}\bm{U}^{\left(m\right)\top}\big)\,\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star}\big\|_{2}\sqrt{\log d}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{U}^{(m)}\bm{U}^{\left(m\right)\top}\big\|\sqrt{\log d}

(193)

and

\big\|\bm{\theta}^{\tau}-\bm{\theta}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}\big\|_{2}\lesssim\big\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{U}^{(m)}\bm{U}^{\left(m\right)\top}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}\sqrt{\log d}.

(194)

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{T}_{m,:,:}^{\star}\times_{3}\big(\bm{\theta}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{\theta}^{\tau}\big)\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{s\in\left[r\right]}\big|\lambda_{s}^{\star}\big\langle\overline{\bm{u}}_{s}^{\star},\bm{\theta}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{\theta}^{\tau}\big\rangle\big|\big\|\overline{\bm{U}}^{\star}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\overline{\bm{U}}^{\star}\big\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\big\|\overline{\bm{U}}^{\star}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{U}^{(m)}\bm{U}^{\left(m\right)\top}\big\|\sqrt{\log d}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{loo}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d}}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\ll\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d}}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}

where we have used Lemma
5.8
as well as the conditions that
\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{loo}}\ll 1
,
\big\|\overline{\bm{U}}^{\star}\big\|\lesssim 1
and
\big\|\overline{\bm{U}}^{\star}\big\|_{2,\infty}\lesssim\sqrt{\mu r/d}
.

Putting the above bounds together, we arrive at

\displaystyle\left\|\big(\bm{M}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{M}^{\star\tau}\big)_{m,:}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\bm{T}_{m,:,:}^{\star}\times_{3}\big(\bm{\theta}^{\tau,\left(m\right)}-\bm{\theta}^{\tau}\big)\big\|_{2}+\big\|\bm{C}_{m,:}^{\tau}\big\|_{2}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\log d}{d}}\,\lambda_{\max}^{\star}.

(195)

We therefore conclude that
